Disclaimer. This site is updated as additional source documents are ingested and reviewed. Its records and calculations are preliminary and open to correction. Additional documents, stakeholder input, and independent review can surface missing information, errors, or context and improve accuracy over time; they may support more complete calculations but do not by themselves verify a record. Inviting people to examine the underlying sources and share corrections is part of the purpose of this work. These are preliminary, document-based observations, not allegations or findings that any person, office, contractor or other entity acted unlawfully. A flag, unmatched record, repeated amount or title, price or location outlier, or missing document is a prompt for review; it does not establish intent, responsibility, benefit, overpayment, duplicate scope or wrongdoing. Names and geographic or district associations indicate possible coverage only, not sponsorship, authorship, control, receipt or personal involvement. Source documents, data completeness, parsing, matching, categories, timing and boundaries may affect results. Verify cited primary records and seek relevant context before drawing or publishing conclusions.

Verified FY2027 invitations to bid

The sources establish invitations and scheduled bid openings. Actual bid openings and FY2027 awards remain unverified.

What the procurement rules allow

Section 12 of the approved implementing rules for RA No. 12009 allows Early Procurement Activities after submission of the NEP, subject to an approved indicative Annual Procurement Plan and the applicable guidelines. The procurement must stop short of award until the appropriate funding is approved and effective and the final procurement plan is approved.

Approved implementing rules, section 12

PhilGEPS v2 historical title matches

The archive includes postings up to 2025-09-30. These title matches flag earlier procurement records for scope review. Establishing a link to FY2027 requires a current procurement record and funding evidence.

Coverage and matching method

Budget coverage includes the available named FY2027 lines for DPWH, farm-to-market roads, health facilities and NIA irrigation. It includes positive SCR allocations and keeps each budget stage separate.

The search compared titles against the notice title, award title and line-item name in 18,515,260 PhilGEPS v2 rows. It matches whole normalized titles after removing accents, case and punctuation. Each title and bid reference pair retains one source row, preferring notice-title evidence. Verify the agency, work scope and location, especially where a short title names only a facility. Archived amounts retain the dataset’s reported values and historical award dates may contain source errors.
